parent
6b9a0f01be621bed7a5514a174cd77e5f96670c2 [ diff ] 
Validate implicit anchors against AnchorMap in GetPositionAnchorElement

GetPositionAnchorElement() returned element->ImplicitAnchorElement()
directly for StylePositionAnchor::Type::kAuto without checking whether
the implicit anchor is an acceptable anchor in anchor_map (unlike
Type::kName, AnchorEvaluatorImpl::ResolveAnchorReference(), and
LayoutBox::AcceptableImplicitAnchor()). As a result, a non-acceptable
or cross-document implicit anchor could be passed to
AnchorPositionVisibilityObserver::MonitorAnchor(), attaching
an IntersectionObserver to another document's element during layout.

Validate Type::kAuto implicit anchors via anchor_map->AnchorReference()
in GetPositionAnchorElement().

[TTC] Make the TTC FRE dialog tab-modal

Converts the TTC FRE dialog from a window-modal dialog to a tab-modal
dialog using TabDialogManager.

Key changes:
- Anchor TtcFreDialog to a TabInterface instead of a NativeWindow,
  ensuring the dialog is scoped to the active tab and only shown when
  the tab can display modal UI.
- Automatically activate and focus the dialog when its associated tab is
  activated.
- Open the "Learn more" link in a new foreground tab rather than a new
  window.
- Move TtcFreDialog unit tests to browser tests to accommodate the
  TabInterface dependency and update interactive tests for tab switching
  behavior.

[Shutdown] Model browser-test reopen lifetimes

Tests that intentionally close the final browser and reopen a window
need to keep the application alive explicitly. A BROWSER-origin
registration alone retains the process but does not prevent last-window
closure from starting shutdown.

Use a test-specific process keep-alive origin and a shared
process/profile lifetime guard for generic close/reopen sequences. Model
Ash's zero-browser lifetime with its native process keep-alive.

[Perf] Defer view inflation in RenameExtensionDialogCoordinator

Extract R.layout.download_rename_extension_custom_dialog inflation
and mRenameExtensionDialogModel initialization into a helper method,
and defer calling it from the RenameExtensionDialogCoordinator
constructor to showDialog() when
ChromeFeatureList.sClankStartupTabOptimizations is enabled. Guard
dismissDialog() on mRenameExtensionDialogModel != null so dismissing
or destroying the coordinator before showDialog() is a no-op, and add
RenameExtensionDialogCoordinatorUnitTest.

[iOS] Add the new tab button to the shared toolbar buttons

While iPhone Duo is folded, a vertical sidebar will show a new tab
button with the toolbar's control buttons. This CL adds it to the
shared toolbar button pipeline, so that the sidebar gets it like the
other buttons:
- ToolbarButtonFactory makes it, a plain plus.
- ToolbarButtonsMediator sends whether it's enabled (adding tabs can be
  disallowed by policy) and its long-press menu, the same as the App
  Bar's, records its metrics, and opens the new tab, incognito in an
  incognito browser.
- ToolbarButtonActions forwards its taps and records menu triggers.

The sidebar's VerticalSidebarViewController (next CL) is its only
consumer: it replaces the App Bar while folded in the Next IA, and
also serves the Legacy IA. The Next IA toolbars ignore the button, so
there's no behavior change.

[iOS] Let Legacy toolbars force-hide individual buttons

While iPhone Duo is folded, a vertical sidebar will show the toolbar's
control buttons, so the Legacy toolbars need to hide theirs meanwhile
without losing their usual visibility rules.

This CL adds:
- `forceHidden` on LegacyToolbarButton. A force-hidden button doesn't
  take its named layout guide, and releases it if it had it, so that
  another button can own it.
- `-setControlButtonsHiddenForVerticalSidebar:` on the Legacy toolbar
  view controllers, which force-hides all their buttons. Their views
  must be loaded. The bottom toolbar also hides its button row, so it
  takes no height without the omnibox, and tells its height delegate.
  The top toolbar lets the location bar span its width when it isn't
  split (compact height).

Nothing calls it yet, so there's no behavior change.

tree
71608194b3e08012314996b69c89a171d31890aa
parent
9cecf2acfaf4e7dded415b3929ef1977fbc0eece [ diff ] 
WebUI: Decompress resources directly into the data pipe

When WebUIDirectResourceDecompression is enabled, WebUI resources that
the renderer serves unmodified (no template replacements or byte range)
are decompressed directly into a payload-sized Mojo data pipe rather
than into an intermediate buffer that is then copied. This avoids the
intermediate buffer's allocation, first-touch page faults, and copy.

On a low-end 32-bit Android device (MediaTek MT6769V, Android Go), a
micro-benchmark modeling both approaches shows this reduces the time to
decompress and send the NTP's 56 resources from 52.6ms to 44.5ms (-15%),
and all 3136 compressed resources in resources.pak from 558ms to 487ms
(-13%).

Embedders that do not implement ContentClient::LoadDataResourceInto()
continue to use the existing path.

[iOS] Fix popup menu IPHs' leading checks in RTL

Popup menu IPHs are leading aligned when their anchor is in the left
half of the screen or menu: the tools menu IPHs when NextIA is enabled,
and the overflow menu IPHs (e.g. the Level Up payment methods step).
Bubble alignments are in leading and trailing terms though, while the
anchors' UIKit frames aren't mirrored, so in RTL this pointed the
bubbles the wrong way.

This CL adds `bubble_util::IsInLeadingHalf()`, which flips for RTL, and
uses it for all these checks.

Screenshots (iPad, Hebrew, Next IA, tools menu IPH):
Before: http://screen/iinbQorCtmXxyiD
After: http://screen/36j5eRjnL8jaQqy

b9c4849040d1d0cbdb08ba06b617e52901d2122f
parent
a3dd96b34737a4e26809f253aaf09b1991839af3 [ diff ] 
WebMCP: Group a ScriptTool's natural language properties [2/N]

The WebMCP explainer's new tool shape moves a tool's title, description,
input and output schemas and annotations into an optional
`NaturalLanguageProperties` dictionary, so that a tool can be registered
for use by web content only, without describing itself to an agent.
`blink.mojom.ScriptTool` keeps these as top-level fields, with a required
`description`, so it can't represent such a tool.

Move these fields into a new nullable `NaturalLanguageProperties` mojom
struct on `ScriptTool`, mirroring the IDL. The next CL uses a null
`natural_language_properties` for `window.toolManager` tools registered
without them. Readers treat a tool without natural language properties
as having no description, schemas or annotations.

This is a pure refactor with no behavior change: `document.modelContext`
and declarative tools always send natural language properties.

`input_schema` and `output_schema` stay optional for now because
`document.modelContext` allows tools to omit them. A TODO tracks making
them required, to match the IDL, once the final API ships.

NO_IFTTT=Only how the ScriptTool mojom is read changed; the extracted proto is unchanged, so the iOS extraction code is unaffected.

[BottomSheet] Avoid caching SheetState.SCROLLING for IME animation
📋 Metadata
commit
299dd72498e6c11ba2414ca4e86b17c273e52769
[ log ]
author
Fiaz Muhammad <mfiaz@google.com>
Thu Nov 26 20:48:23 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 26 20:48:23 2026
tree
458f6ef8bd6fac745ad891ea4c49dba1082ab7d9
parent
7f218854f7a2e8c53f8f2b89ca61fbde8b5faf41 [ diff ] 
[BottomSheet] Avoid caching SheetState.SCROLLING for IME animation

Previously, BottomSheetMediator.maybeCacheStateForImeAnimation()
unconditionally cached mCurrentState. When an IME animation started
while the sheet was in SheetState.SCROLLING, caching SCROLLING could
later cause
BottomSheetCoordinator.setSheetState(SheetState.SCROLLING, false) to
hit an AssertionError on debug builds or skip resettling on release
builds when the keyboard dismissed.

Skip caching when isScrolling() is true (preventing an active drag
interrupting a settle animation from caching a stale mTargetState).
Otherwise resolve the state to cache to mTargetState when mCurrentState
is SheetState.SCROLLING (so an in-flight settle animation preserves its
destination state), and skip caching without acquiring a keyboard token
when no valid non-scrolling state is available (such as SheetState.NONE
or SheetState.SCROLLING).

[Perf] Replace Map with SparseArray in ManageSyncSettings

Replace Map<Integer, ChromeSwitchPreference> with
SparseArray<ChromeSwitchPreference> for
ManageSyncSettings#mSyncTypeSwitchPreferencesMap. Using SparseArray
avoids boxing UserSelectableType int keys into Integer objects and
reduces HashMap entry allocation overhead.

[🌷] Deflake ContextualSearch FindInPage overlay test

When FindToolbar opens, it focuses mFindQuery and asynchronously opens
the soft keyboard under SOFT_INPUT_ADJUST_RESIZE. Previously,
testTriggeringContextualSearchHidesFindInPageOverlay clicked "search"
immediately after FindToolbar became visible without waiting for the IME
resize or the subsequent scrollIntoView() compositor frame commit,
causing DOMUtils.clickNode() to compute shifted bounds or dispatch taps
before Viz committed the scroll offset (clicking "United" or
non-triggering elements above "search").

Wait for SoftKeyboardCondition and compositor frame presentation before
and after scrolling "search" into view, capture mPanel's peek sequence
before dispatching the click in clickWordNode() to eliminate a
post-click sequence race, and null-guard mPanel in tearDown().

[structured metrics] Use base::ByteSize for storage limits

Replace base::SysInfo::AmountOfFreeDiskSpace() with
base::SysInfo::AmountOfDiskSpace(), which returns the available space as
a base::ByteSize. A failed query is still treated as zero available
space.

Also use base::ByteSize for the in-memory and on-disk storage limits:
- StorageManagerConfig now holds base::ByteSize fields.
- The ArenaEventBuffer constructor takes its maximum size as a
  base::ByteSize.
- The minimum sizes are base::KiB() constants.
- A new ScaleByRatio() helper applies the feature-param ratios, clamping
  negative results to zero as the previous std::max() did.

This is a refactor with no intended behavior change.

Guard PaymentRequest re-entrant teardown and tear down on discard
📋 Metadata
commit
ec77c9e0a38437c56c4932774610d1a0d9c10ee1
[ log ]
author
Slobodan Pejic <slobodan@chromium.org>
Thu Nov 26 20:41:44 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 26 20:41:44 2026
tree
3c212dd9fc116125245a825bd7414350022221f3
parent
e7e8dd9e77723d07e8bb8ee51a76e77e69f03ebf [ diff ] 
Guard PaymentRequest re-entrant teardown and tear down on discard

When a Payment Handler responds, PaymentRequestDialogView hides the
handler's WebView (via ShowProcessingSpinner() or ShowErrorMessage()).
Hiding that opaque Aura window can synchronously transition an occluded
merchant tab to VISIBLE via WindowOcclusionTracker and trigger a
synchronous early RenderFrameHost swap when NavigationController has
needs_reload_ set (for example after kWebContentsDiscard or a crashed
subframe on a hidden tab). That synchronously destroys PaymentRequest
and ServiceWorkerPaymentApp while both are still on the call stack.

Fix the synchronous re-entrancy hazards:
- Guard PaymentRequest::OnPaymentResponseAvailable() with a WeakPtr
  across delegate_->ShowProcessingSpinner().
- Clear ServiceWorkerPaymentApp::delegate_ via std::exchange before
  invoking OnInstrumentDetailsReady/Error in OnPaymentAppResponse().
- Guard PaymentRequestDialogView::ShowErrorMessage(),
  ResizeDialogWindow(), and HideProcessingSpinner() against re-entrant
  teardown across view_stack_->Push().

Also prevent zombie PaymentRequests on discarded documents under
kWebContentsDiscard (which preserves the active RenderFrameHost and its
DocumentServices until reload):
- Override PaymentRequest::WasDiscarded() to record ABORT_REASON_OTHER
  and tear down via ResetAndDeleteThis() when WebContents::WasDiscarded()
  is true.
- Reject new PaymentRequests in CreatePaymentRequest() and
  PaymentRequest::Init() when the hosting WebContents was already
  discarded.

📋 Metadata

41a56d67fe23ef80ed09351ccf796967038f96e3
[ log ]
author
Nancy Xiao <nancylanxiao@google.com>
Thu Nov 26 20:38:54 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 26 20:38:54 2026
tree
caa10c46a258cc9944083c3ef62003f2a9dde4fb
parent
08a0029dfc90958c573ffb44dc0b9a80db057ccd [ diff ] 
Allow async upload for downloads that are not obfuscated

Downloads were always forced to finish their content upload before a
verdict was used, ignoring a verdict sent ahead of the upload in the
X-Goog-Upload-Header-Cep-Response header. This was added because an
obfuscated download is deobfuscated in place once a verdict allows it,
which would replace the file while its content is still being uploaded.

Only force the synchronous upload when the downloaded file is actually
obfuscated. Downloads that are not obfuscated (e.g. files >= 50MB) are
not rewritten after the verdict, so they now use the early verdict and
upload their content afterwards.

This is a stop-gap: obfuscated downloads keep the existing behavior.

tree
f51d0b28392412163c6e15dbcedf1797a65bcf1c
parent
f5b3297723fa8f69baaee08f194950af2ed2688b [ diff ] 
[iOS][Gemini] Record session metrics on Chrome-initiated dismissals

Record `GeminiSessionHandler` end-of-session metrics when Chrome
dismisses the Gemini UI without an SDK dismissal callback.

- Migration on (`kIOSGeminiBottomSheetMigration` enabled): Adds session
  dismissal metrics for Chrome-initiated dismissals that bypass
  `didRequestDismissal()`, such as the zero-state close button,
  zero-state swipe-down, dimming scrim tap, toolbar/app-bar toggle,
  switching to Incognito, Lens Overlay, or multi-window dismissal.
- Migration off (`kIOSGeminiBottomSheetMigration` disabled): Adds
  session dismissal metrics for Chrome-initiated dismissals via
  `GeminiBrowserAgent::DismissFloaty()` (such as switching to
  Incognito, toolbar/app-bar toggle, Lens Overlay, or multi-window
  dismissal) that call `ResetGemini()` without triggering
  `UIDidDisappearWithClientID:serverID:`.

Calling `GeminiSessionHandler` from `onFloatyDismiss()` is temporary
until `GeminiSessionHandler` merges into `GeminiContainerMediator` and
`GeminiSessionMetricsManager`. Until then, dismissal metrics cannot
migrate in isolation to `GeminiSessionMetricsManager` because
`GeminiSessionHandler` owns the session state required to record these
histograms (session start time, first-session state, prompt count,
first-prompt state, and feature engagement tracker).

[Enterprise] Add datapol annotations to browser_events.proto

Add copybara datapol semantic annotations (ST_USER_CONTENT, ST_EMAIL_ID,
and ST_USERNAME) to unannotated URL, source/destination, email, and
username fields across Chrome reporting event messages in
components/enterprise/common/proto/synced/browser_events.proto.

Downstream consumers sanitize these event protos with standard
annotation-driven proto sanitizers before logging. Without datapol
annotations on fields such as UrlInfo.url, event source/destination,
top-level and referrer URLs, recipient_emails, profile_user_name, and
login/breach usernames, sensitive end-user email addresses and URLs
bypass automatic redaction unless cleared manually per field.

Test: Verified Copybara sync_events_to_google3 and protosanitizer tests

parent
942768b245fa88c7b6dd5a69f2e2db131c03d17e [ diff ] 
[Android] Fix DecorationTabTitle bubble re-attachment and hiding

DecorationTabTitle::CreateAndShowTabBubble() unconditionally called
layer_->AddChild(tab_bubble_outer_circle_layer_) on every setBounds()
call, forcing cc::slim::Layer to remove and re-insert the bubble every
frame. HideTabBubble() also called SetHideLayerAndSubtree(true), which
CreateAndShowTabBubble() never cleared, leaving a hidden bubble
permanently invisible if shown again.

Attach tab_bubble_outer_circle_layer_ once in CreateTabBubble(), call
SetHideLayerAndSubtree(false) in CreateAndShowTabBubble(), and add a
unit test covering repeated setBounds() and hide/re-show transitions.

[Android] Avoid ContentLayer child thrashing and duplicate GetTab JNI

ContentLayer::SetProperties called RemoveAllChildren() and AddChild()
unconditionally on every frame, detaching and re-attaching the entire
WebContents layer subtree even when children were unchanged.
StaticTabSceneLayer::UpdateTabLayer also fetched TabAndroid via
GetTab(id) and then triggered a second GetTab(id) reverse-JNI call
inside ContentLayer::SetProperties -> GetLiveLayer(id).

Reconcile ContentLayer's children in place without detaching unchanged
layers, replace ContentLayer::SetProperties's can_use_live_layer flag
with a nullable live_layer parameter (passing nullptr when live layers
are disallowed in StaticTabSceneLayer and ToolbarSwipeSceneLayer),
CHECK_NE(TabAndroid::kInvalidTabId) in TabContentManager::GetTab(),
drop the unused virtual qualifier on TabContentManager::GetLiveLayer(),
and add regression tests.

[selection] Follow this tab's panel and clear stale chips

Selection updates now follow this tab's side panel, or the floating
window while this tab is active, instead of any window's panel.

- Improvement: deselecting while the panel is closed pushes an empty
  update on reopen to clear the stale text chip.
- Improvement: background tabs no longer push when another tab's panel
  toggles, and switching tabs tells the floating window that tab's
  selection.
- Existing behavior: opening the panel on a tab with no selection pushes
  nothing, and reopening pushes any kept selection, also to a new
  instance.
- `SelectionContextSender` no longer starts an invocation; it only
  updates this tab's panel while it's shown.

f8a48d2b9d935183a6d955e5bad7f8fbe26b0f15
parent
c26e6d73261c89771c064f62b24fb51954a16009 [ diff ] 
Recompute visual overflow when border-shape's stroke side may change

border-shape strokes with the first border side whose style isn't none.
Changing that side from hidden or 0px solid to none keeps every used
border width at zero, so no layout runs and visual overflow wasn't
recomputed, while the stroke switched to a much wider side. The stale
ink overflow let the box paint over content without being seen by
occlusion checks and hit-test culling.

Recompute visual overflow on border width or style changes when
border-shape is set, and update paint properties in that case so the
inner border-shape clip follows.

AsanBrp: Fix aligned non-BRP allocations on Windows
📋 Metadata

9a2bd69df9748cda27a35b333db48c21373ff6b2
[ log ]
author
Ben Bamesberger <benb@microsoft.com>
Thu Nov 26 20:19:32 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 26 20:19:32 2026
tree
5e510d48a35b4fc2ec3804eedc1077ee13149721
parent
08124ba2e794d5e1c24ee68aef3094d8d44a3bfd [ diff ] 
AsanBrp: Fix aligned non-BRP allocations on Windows

AsanBrp removes the BRP mark from allocations made by non-BRP
PartitionRoot-s. On Windows, ASan does not intercept _aligned_malloc(),
so an aligned allocation from such a root returns a pointer into the
middle of the underlying ASan allocation. PartitionAllocHook() then
looks for the mark relative to that pointer rather than the start of the
allocation, which fails the CHECK.

Find the mark from the start of the allocation, as reported by
__sanitizer_get_allocated_begin(). Platforms where ASan intercepts the
aligned allocation functions are unaffected, because the pointer is
already the start of the allocation.

[Tab Group Sync] Accept opening source and report status on group open

Opening a synced tab group from Android UI always records the revisit
surface as the source and returns no status. The Restore Tabs promo
needs to attribute opens to the promo and fall back to ungrouped
restore if the group does not open.

Extend the group-open entry point to accept an opening source and
report whether a closed group opened, pausing local sync observation
during creation while preserving any existing observation pause, and
update existing UI callers to pass the revisit opening source.

[Android] Disable static thumbnail lookup in StaticLayout
📋 Metadata
commit
10360f2b7d1c44de043e23f0a4abb8e67ea09c05
[ log ]
author
Calder Kitagawa <ckitagawa@chromium.org>
Thu Nov 26 20:14:39 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 26 20:14:39 2026
tree
90d014036d8e0a65a37b5955cdddd41701e28bf6
parent
8c488084655068ee2c0247e3e64e65e840885b8a [ diff ] 
[Android] Disable static thumbnail lookup in StaticLayout

StaticLayout always displays the active tab's live WebContents or
Android View content overlay (NativePage/SadTab) and already suppresses
ETC1 disk reads via primaryTabId. However, ContentLayer::SetProperties
unconditionally called TabContentManager::GetStaticLayer whenever the
live layer was not drawing, forcing StaticLayout to pin NativePage
thumbnails in ThumbnailCache and StaticTabSceneLayer to run an
UpdateVisibleIds({id}, id) DCHECK workaround.

Gate static thumbnail lookup in ContentLayer::SetProperties and
StaticTabSceneLayer on a can_use_thumbnail flag (false for StaticLayout;
true for HubLayout, NewTabAnimationLayout, and ToolbarSwipeLayout).
Remove the UpdateVisibleIds DCHECK workaround and LayerDraws helper from
StaticTabSceneLayer, and stop pinning NativePage thumbnails in
StaticLayout.

Change-Id: Ibba014cf9f4e3e7652d5801f2b7eee2a6a6a6964

activity_reporter: Move the histograms to their own directory

Move the four ActivityReporter.* histograms, and the
InitialDateLastActiveState and PingFreshnessState enums that only they
use, from tools/metrics/histograms/metadata/others/ to a new
tools/metrics/histograms/metadata/activity_reporter/ directory, so that
the activity reporter's owners own its histograms. crrev.com/c/8857818,
the next CL for this bug, edits them.

The new directory's OWNERS are sorin@chromium.org and
noahrose@google.com, and METRIC_REVIEWER_OWNERS lists
sorin@chromium.org for it. Regarding the addition to OWNERS, I confirm
that I completed the go/reviewing-metrics training and quiz.

The histograms and enums are moved unchanged. The LINT.ThenChange()
comments in activity_reporter.h and persisted_data.h now point to the
new enums.xml, and histogram_paths.py regenerated
histograms_xml_files.gni.

Also:
- components/activity_reporter gets a DIR_METADATA that mixes in
  chrome/updater/COMMON_METADATA, matching its OWNERS. These histograms
  list components/activity_reporter/OWNERS as an owner, so they now map
  to the Internals>Updater component.
- The TODO in features.cc names the new histograms.xml.

dedf8ec7a150c4d23c620925d55a76404371aad3 [ diff ] 
Android: Apply the Chrome theme color to WebUI pages

Apply the user's Chrome theme color, color variant, light/dark scheme,
grayscale setting, device theme, and managed theme policy to WebUI
pages (including their prefers-color-scheme media query) and direct
WindowAndroid ColorProvider callers, while keeping regular web content
on Android night mode.

Also remove the old Android ColorProviderBridge path (along with
per-Context ColorProvider keys and the WebUiAndroidTheming flag), which
covered only ~30 Material colors and left the rest stuck on default
blue/grey; WebUI pages now get their full palette unconditionally from
Chrome's ThemeService (defaulting to the OS dynamic color), matching
desktop without keeping a legacy fallback path.

Stop setting ColorProviderSource from //content in
WebContentsAndroid::SetTopLevelNativeWindow() and instead manage it from
the Chrome embedder (matching desktop): regular browser windows adopt
Chrome's ThemeService; Custom Tabs, Popups, AuthTabs, TWAs, Webapps, and
WebAPKs follow only their Activity night mode; and Incognito or
Enterprise Isolated WebContents stay isolated even when hosted in
mixed-mode windows.

Also refresh NTP, Customize Chrome, and Theme Color Picker theme state
on Android when a WebContents' ColorProvider changes (such as after
Activity recreation), and enable ThemeService unit tests on Android.

[PM] Replace FrameNode::IsRendered and ViewportIntersection with 1 enum

commit
c93a67b9a3f0a68164e23cfcce69637cd5652c9e
[ log ]
author

Thu Nov 26 20:04:00 2026
tree
5559400f90c58db674012ec3f095a7ade9847e43
parent
66fe54304d3be4c5d8e845d840f2d16865d5f803 [ diff ] 
[PM] Replace FrameNode::IsRendered and ViewportIntersection with 1 enum

FrameNode previously exposed two separate properties driven by the same
WebContentsObserver::OnFrameVisibilityChanged() IPC:
- IsRendered() (bool, defaulting to false and never set on outermost
  main frames).
- GetViewportIntersection() (3-state ViewportIntersection enum).

Remove IsRendered() and expand ViewportIntersection to 4 values
(kUnknown, kNotRendered, kRenderedOutOfViewport, and kIntersecting) with
no semantic changes:

```
IsRendered | ViewportIntersection | New ViewportIntersection
-----------+----------------------+-------------------------------
true       | kUnknown             | (impossible)*
false      | kUnknown             | kUnknown
true       | kNotIntersecting     | kRenderedOutOfViewport
false      | kNotIntersecting     | kNotRendered
true       | kIntersecting        | kIntersecting
false      | kIntersecting        | kIntersecting
```

Outermost main frames (and orphan SurfaceEmbed roots) do not track
viewport intersection and always return kIntersecting.

* IsRendered true with ViewportIntersection::kUnknown was possible only
  in one corner case, and is now totally impossible. The default
  FrameNode state was ViewportIntersection::kUnknown and IsRendered
  false, and PerformanceManagerTabHelper::OnFrameVisibilityChanged()
  always updated ViewportIntersection (to non-kUnknown) when updating
  IsRendered. The exception was a SurfaceEmbed root whose parent
  disappeared while the child's first visibility update was still in
  transit: this could update IsRendered but leave ViewportIntersection
  unchanged. Since the "is rendered" state is now part of
  ViewportIntersection, it's left unchanged in this orphan state too.

PerformanceManagerTabHelper continues to map Blink's
kRenderedOutOfViewport to kIntersecting when the
kRenderedOutOfViewIsNotVisible feature is disabled.

Bug: None
Test: components_unittests (*FrameNode*, *Decorator*, *Priority*, *PageNode*)
Test: components_browsertests (*PerformanceManager*)
Test: browser_tests (*FrameNodeImplBrowserTest*)

Record the source of precomputed :hover text decorations

This CL records the element whose precomputed :hover style changes its
text decoration as the source of the decoration. The paint code will use
the source to key the color of the decoration across descendants & text
fragments, so that the compositor can change the color while the element
is hovered.

[base] Prevent duplicate shutdown Signal() in OperationsController
📋 Metadata
commit
93f49be04615fecf9a1b22b9191db5fb776080e6
[ log ]
author

Thu Nov 26 19:59:55 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 26 19:59:55 2026
tree
cbc93c1fb0bf30b3ec54e0c2f2f61fc2fa8105f9
parent
01063b4a54c2a7bbf2d19ad64aef8c907208ae55 [ diff ] 
[base] Prevent duplicate shutdown Signal() in OperationsController

OperationsController::TryBeginOperation() previously incremented and
decremented state_and_count_ even after
ShutdownAndWaitForZeroOperations() was called. If an exiting
OperationToken transitioned the count from 1 -> 0 and was preempted
before completing shutdown_complete_.Signal(), a concurrent rejected
TryBeginOperation() could bump 0 -> 1 -> 0 and emit a second Signal().
That could wake the shutting-down thread early and allow the
OperationsController (e.g. inside GuardedTaskPoster) to be destroyed
while the first thread was still in Signal().

Ensure shutdown_complete_.Signal() is called at most once by preventing
post-zero rejected TryBeginOperation() calls from transitioning back to
zero, and avoid unwinding rejected attempts when shutting down before
StartAcceptingOperations().

Thu Nov 26 19:58:38 2026
tree
32918b258bdbb37a3798fa83ca05fb76916003a0
parent
6dcbe34701d6836965d91295eb3a3484b5ba5385 [ diff ] 
[TabStrip] Re-anchor Shift+Click when anchor is no longer selected

When an external tab switch (e.g., closing the active tab onto an
unselected neighbor, keyboard shortcut, or Tab Search) clears
multi-selection via TabModel#setIndex(), TabMultiSelectHelper's
mAnchorTabId still pointed at the old anchor tab. A subsequent
Shift+Click then spanned from the stale anchor instead of the newly
active tab.

Validate tabModel.isTabMultiSelected(mAnchorTabId) in
TabMultiSelectHelper#handleShiftClick() before destructive Shift+Click
clears the previous selection. If the old anchor is no longer selected
(or was closed), re-anchor mAnchorTabId to the currently active tab.

[tips] Refresh Magic Stack opt-in eligibility on notification changes

Adds a process-wide NotificationPermissionChangeNotifier notified by
NotificationPermissionChangeReceiver (OS settings broadcasts) and
NotificationPermissionController (Android 13+ runtime prompt), and
registers TipsNotificationPermissionObserver in ChromeApplicationImpl
to keep TIPS_NOTIFICATIONS_OPT_IN_ELIGIBLE fresh across warm resumes.

Disqualifying changes (app notifications blocked or TIPS_V2 unblocked)
clear the pref synchronously so cold broadcasts do not depend on an
async channel lookup outliving BroadcastReceiver.onReceive().

Automated Clean-Up: Remove unused UKM <aggregation> for Memory.TabFootprint

An audit of UkmHistory_{1d,7d} over the past 12 months (b/584304583)
found 0 dashboard or F1 queries for these <aggregation> metrics (all
landed >300 days ago). Removing unused aggregations reduces our UKM
pipeline and storage costs. Note that UKM event-metrics collection and
non-aggregated tables are unaffected; only aggregated UkmHistory
entries are removed, and they will no longer be accessible on the UKM
dashboard. If needed in the future, this change can be reverted.

If this change looks good, please Code-Review+1. If your team
queries these in a private script, which our audit misses, please
comment to keep them; or we will land this cleanup after 5 business
days as UKM OWNERS.

[startup] Skip relative file fixup for direct-launch scheme URLs

Arguments that arrive through the direct-launch scheme are untrusted,
but they went through the relative file fixup that is meant for trusted
command-line arguments before ValidateLaunchUrlWebSafe.

Parse the stripped argument with GURL and validate it right away, as the
macOS and in-browser handlers already do. Inner URLs without a scheme
are now rejected instead of being fixed up.

This change was written with AI assistance.

[WebAuthn] Ambient UI displays domains for affiliated passwords

Display the affiliated origin for passwords when present. This is
necessary context when a password credential is being shown that does
not match the caller's origin.

It is used in the same way as the IdP origin in FedCM on the bubble.

When a password has an affiliated origin, it replaces the provider
in the unexpanded bubble. If the user expands the bubble, the
credential will show both the affiliated origin and the credential
provider.

This change collapses `FederatedCredentialRow` into its base class
`CredentialRow` since the extra origin line is no longer distinct.

[viz] Defer Graphite backend flush for CopyOutput to next submit

Add GraphiteSharedContext::ForceFlushBackendOnNextSubmit() and use it
in SkiaOutputSurfaceImplOnGpu::CopyOutput() instead of calling
submitAndFlushBackend() immediately.

This avoids an extra Graphite Context::submit() mid-frame while still
ensuring that the next submit (e.g. SkiaOutputDevice::Submit()) flushes
the backend (such as Dawn D3D11 with d3d11_delay_flush_to_gpu) so async
readback polling does not stall on overlay-only frames.

Allow --omnibox-everywhere for the isolated browser

The --omnibox-everywhere flag is used for directly triggering the
OmniboxEverywhere (aka "Search with Chrome") floating searchbox. The
switch has no value and only affects Chrome startup so that it displays
this rather than the browser window directly.

Without it on the allowlist, the taskbar pin and Start Menu shortcut
open the Profile Picker or a browser window when Chrome runs isolated.

tree
29074cec4e17ae9d69df9991caacd30502697f50
parent
1646f344397f81be717b2662f3f627db173a044c [ diff ] 
mojo: channel_linux: Test concurrent upgrade offers

Add ChannelUpgradeTest.ConcurrentOffers to exercise the shared
memory upgrade protocol.

Channel upgrades with ipcz are only enabled on Android and fuzzer builds
on Linux. The test is therefore Android-only. Enabling eventfd upgrades
on ChromeOS and Linux would require finding a backwards-compatible way
to make upgrades.

In the new test each side of a channel offers the upgrade for its
outgoing direction at most once. Normally when ChannelLinux accepts an
offer it immediately sends the reciprocal offer.

The test makes offers concurrently from different threads with these 3
flavours:
* Both sides offer at the same time
* The second side's offer races with the reciprocal offer
* Only one side offers (as with Broker-to-NonBroker channels today)

For each mode, 20 iterations verify that in both directions no offer is
lost and no side ends up half-upgraded. Also check that message order
is preserved despite the split between socket and shared memory.

This prepares for offering upgrades from both sides of channels between
two non-brokers.

tree
ce6426adbc334b1d87b5f522f27e927036f7f153
parent
0d2cb95868812893e009116538c0813fda79a497 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Move Next IA toolbar button actions into ToolbarButtonActions

This CL moves the taps, menu haptics and tab grid menu of the Next IA
toolbar's control buttons out of ToolbarViewController into a
ToolbarButtonActions helper, so that every surface showing the buttons
behaves and records metrics the same way.

ToolbarButtonActions holds no state of the surfaces showing the
buttons, so that they don't have to keep it in sync:
- ToolbarButtonsMediator records the tap metrics, including the NTP
  variants and the tab grid home action, from its active WebState,
  through `-recordUserActionsForButtonTapped:`.
- ToolbarButtonsMediator gives the tab grid menu to the actions, as a
  ToolbarTabGridMenuConsumer, instead of to each consumer.
- ToolbarButtonsConsumer no longer gets the Start Surface state, which
  only these metrics used.

The recognizer of taps on the disabled back and forward buttons, which
shows the actor's interaction-limited snackbar, also comes from
ToolbarButtonActions, which gets the ActorOverlayCommands handler.

The tab grid button now uses `UIButton.menu`, like the back and
forward buttons, instead of its own UIContextMenuInteraction. A
deferred element gives it the actions' latest tab grid menu when it
opens. Its menu now looks like the other toolbar menus, without the
custom lifted preview, and plays the same haptic feedback as the back
and forward buttons' menus.

ToolbarButtonType moves to its own header, shared by the consumer, the
mutator and the actions.

Add LifetimeAssert.scopedDisable() and use in ProfileKeyedMap

Profile-scoped objects owned by ProfileKeyedMap live until Profile
destruction, which does not happen between batched instrumentation
tests. Migrating such services to JniUniquePtr (which registers a
LifetimeAssert) caused end-of-test LifetimeAssert failures.

Also migrates SearchEngineService to JniUniquePtr as an initial user.

a69eb6928cb8627598693a84156b07ce869116f3

author

tree
822ebd116fedb75d6f33e7cdad25bd268d89d122
parent
8f20ed8709e438139324788ee466474f0d437073 [ diff ] 
[a11y] Guard AXBlockFlowData against null LayoutObject fragments

This is a short-term patch to avoid the crashes. The long-term fix will
likely involve calling RemoveSubtree when DisplayLock is locked and will
be tracked in crbug.com//590790196

Issue summary: When a content-visibility: auto element transitions from
unlocked to locked after layout, cached AXInlineTextBoxes can remain
inside the locked subtree while layout is skipped. If an inline
descendant is later removed inside that locked block,
LayoutText::WillBeDestroyed() nulls its FragmentItem's LayoutObject
without re-laying out the block flow. Serializing a surviving sibling
AXInlineTextBox then rebuilds AXBlockFlowData, hitting a null-key
HashTable DCHECK in ProcessBoxFragment() and a CHECK_EQ failure in
ComputeNeighborOnLine(). Generated text fragment items can also lack a
LayoutObject and trigger the same null-key lookup in
ProcessBoxFragment().

Bug: 590790196,589705850
AX-Relnotes: n/a.

tree
d350bc1b3d92fb26267fd94b477346b9be5e2ac8
parent
598b0eb3049069ed2b6eceb7254bc8d9613a2ce4 [ diff ] 
[FedCM] Guard Android mediator re-entrancy, metric, and dismissal

Fixes three pre-existing state-guard bugs in Android's
`AccountSelectionMediator` (and `AccountSelectionCoordinator`):

- Check `mWasDismissed` and whether `mHeaderType` changed after
  `mDelegate.onAccountSelected()` in `showAccountsInternal()` and
  `onAccountSelected()`, so synchronous dismissal or error dialogs are
  not overwritten by `showVerifySheet()`. Move
  `DisclosureDialogResult.CONTINUE` recording out of `showVerifySheet()`
  into `onAccountSelected()` so synchronous completion, dismissal, or
  error dialogs from `REQUEST_PERMISSION_MODAL` still record `CONTINUE`
  once instead of dropping it or recording `DESTROY`. Desktop
  `FedCmAccountSelectionView` post-selection state-change guards are
  left for a separate follow-up CL.
- Record `Blink.FedCm.Button.LoadingDialogResult` in `showAccounts()`
  only when transitioning from `HeaderType.LOADING` (matching desktop),
  avoiding duplicate `PROCEED` samples on subsequent `showAccounts()`
  calls, and update `AccountSelectionButtonModeIntegrationTest` to show
  the loading dialog first.
- Guard `showAccounts()`, `showFailureDialog()`, `showLoadingDialog()`,
  `showErrorDialog()`, `showVerifyingDialog()`, and `updateSheet()` when
  `mWasDismissed` is true, and avoid replacing an already-dismissed
  switched mediator in `AccountSelectionCoordinator.showFailureDialog()`.
  For example, in passive mode `OnAccountSelected()` resets
  `on_dismiss_` in `IdentityDialogController`, so swiping the verifying
  sheet marks the mediator dismissed without completing the request, and
  a subsequent token error still calls `showErrorDialog()`. The four
  dialog entry points with dismiss callbacks share
  `notifyDismissedIfAlreadyDismissed()` to notify
  `mDelegate.onDismissed(OTHER)` before returning false, preserving
  `IdentityDialogController`'s dismissal contract in both passive and
  active modes, while `showVerifyingDialog()` and `updateSheet()` return
  false without mutating sheet state.

2ec15de95384061ff39042ff6ecbc79cf74a2eb9
parent
04371d3407119c1e0b6ad6a9ae58f4dd76f0f0e3 [ diff ] 
[TTC] Skip redundant restricted page refreshes before navigation

Previously, every user speech turn called RefreshPageContext() even when
the current page had already been reported as restricted since the last
navigation. That re-sent identical RestrictedPageContext updates on
unsupported schemes, crashed tabs, and enterprise blocks, and re-ran
full APC extraction on safety-list and eligibility blocks before
discarding it.

Track the last delivered PageRestrictionReason on TtcPageContextMonitor
(cleared on OnPageContentReset() or any non-restricted update), return
early in RefreshPageContext() when set, and deduplicate repeated
identical restricted deliveries in NotifyPageContextFetched().

Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*PageContextMonitor*
Bug: b:585618943

f88e585b7a8dc7c154e6ba7af0fe2708004950d1
[ log ]
author

tree
67f5bf557ea18bc4c7cec88488543a58cf0a94b3
parent
1d46a06c70adf1da4de201a96c7117e294e1a398 [ diff ] 
3pp: Work around GraalVM 25i G1 SoftReference clearing bug

Oracle GraalVM 25i with --gc=G1 constant-folds SoftReference.clock to 0
(https://github.com/oracle/graal/issues/15215), causing young GCs to
immediately clear all SoftReferences regardless of free heap space. In
lint-graalvm, a young GC during CoreJarHandler.<init> evicted
android.jar's cached entry map mid-construction and left android.jar
appearing empty, causing intermittent SupportAnnotationUsage, NewApi,
and SystemPermissionTypo failures (https://crrev.com/c/8871144).

Bug: 590277979

parent
d0465f127fe7ca987015fc8e8055f4a85c2ea802 [ diff ] 
[tabs] Record tab switch phase durations (2/2)

A tab switch with no saved frame
(Browser.Tabs.TotalSwitchDuration3.NoSavedFrames_Loaded) depends on
running a WasShown task on the renderer main thread [1]. We suspect that
the queueing time of that task explains a large portion of the slow
(>1s) tab switches with no saved frame.

To verify that hypothesis, this CL stack records
Browser.Tabs.NoSavedFramesLoaded.SwitchPhaseDuration.{Phase}, which
splits each Browser.Tabs.TotalSwitchDuration3.NoSavedFrames_Loaded
sample presented by the renderer into 3 phases:
- BrowserMainThread: From tab activation to when the browser main
  thread sends the WasShown IPC to the renderer.
- BrowserToRendererMainThread: From when the WasShown IPC is sent to
  when the renderer main thread starts handling it (IPC and renderer
  main thread queueing).
- RendererMainThreadToPresentation: From when the renderer main thread
  starts handling the WasShown IPC to frame presentation.

- CL 1/2 (https://crrev.com/c/8840941): Send the time when the
  browser main thread sends the WasShown IPC to the renderer.
- CL 2/2 (this CL): Record the phase durations.

This CL (2/2) records the phase durations when the renderer presents a
frame for a loaded tab without a saved frame, using the same buckets as
Browser.Tabs.TotalSwitchDuration3.NoSavedFrames_Loaded [2]. For each tab
switch, the phases add up to the
Browser.Tabs.TotalSwitchDuration3.NoSavedFrames_Loaded sample.

[1] Main-thread WasShown dispatch:
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:content/browser/renderer_host/render_widget_host_impl.cc;l=976-991;drc=a45d13b6914a75312ef731b4852d4e865a7ffc7e
[2] TotalSwitchDuration3 bucket layout:
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/common/page/content_to_visible_time_reporter.cc;l=152-160;drc=a45d13b6914a75312ef731b4852d4e865a7ffc7e

3d213183e4fb71d6b50100ce982ae0b803d8a30b [ diff ] 
[animation-trigger] Copy document triggers before iterating them

This CL fixes a use-after-free in animation triggers. A document's
triggers were iterated in place, on the assumption that acting on a
trigger cannot create new triggers. But setting up a trigger on the
compositor can resolve style-dependent offsets, which can force a
style update that creates triggers. Adding those to the set being
iterated can free the storage that the iteration is still reading.
The triggers are now copied before they are iterated.

720af24ea669872c476f64f8c371b921cae7b7c5
parent
e61d9d645dfce99f45fe25af118f40bb34877f4c [ diff ] 
Fall back to DomCode for access keys on non-Latin layouts

With a non-Latin keyboard layout such as Russian, Shift+Alt+KeyF
produces a Cyrillic character, so
KeyboardEventManager::HandleAccessKey() never matches accesskey="f"
even though browser shortcuts such as Alt+F still work.

When no element matches a non-ASCII character, retry with the US layout
Latin letter or digit corresponding to evt.dom_code (US_A..US_Z,
DIGIT0..DIGIT9), which is preserved on kChar events across platforms.
This aligns with Firefox, which also tries the ASCII character of the
physical key.

Fixed: 40152834

[gcm] Extend FakeGCMServer and FakeMCSServer for reuse

FakeGCMServer and FakeMCSServer currently require an ephemeral MCS port,
crash on bind errors, require a disk storage directory, and only support
raw_data payloads.

Add optional fixed-port binding with non-fatal failure, in-memory
startup, app_data key-value pairs in SendMessage(), sender token lookup,
and HasLoggedInClients() so callers like fake_dmserver can reuse them.

Report App-Bound Encryption state in Chrome reporting

Admins reviewing Chrome browsers and profiles in the Admin Console
cannot currently tell whether Chrome on Windows is protecting browser
data with App-Bound Encryption, or why protection failed to start.

Report the Windows App-Bound Encryption state in both the browser report
(read by the Admin Console browser list and profile status views) and
the profile security signals report (read by the Admin Console profile
list and security signals pipelines), gated behind the default-disabled
`ReportAppBoundEncryptionState` flag. On Windows, enabling this flag
also includes any pending `OSCryptAsync` initialization wait in the
`BrowserContextSignals` collection latency histogram.

0ef837499c0bc9fe45eadc14d40cba0cc2e16cc7
parent
acaaa6b73a6d4f61135ce78b83e0bd33a3f815d6 [ diff ] 
Swap/copy pending updates before executing them in LocalFrameView

This is a short-term fix to prevent iterator invalidation when
executing pending updates in
LocalFrameView::ExecuteAllPendingUpdates(),
ExecutePendingStickyUpdates(), and
ExecutePendingScrollMarkerSelectionUpdates().

This patch copies pending transform/opacity updates into local weak
sets before iterating, skips destroyed objects via
LayoutObject::GetWeak(), and re-checks eligibility before executing
updates. This patch similarly swaps pending_sticky_updates_ and
pending_scroll_marker_selection_updates_ into local containers before
iterating and skips detached entries. This approach mirrors the fix for
ExecutePendingSnapUpdates() in https://crrev.com/c/8685261.

Fixed: 589815785

actor: Move implicit origin grant to ProcessNextFromDispatcher
📋 Metadata

acaaa6b73a6d4f61135ce78b83e0bd33a3f815d6

tree
a2e330ed3afa5da19d05d4220d73a92dccb3380e
parent
13af4a67bf34aadfee669456d6e86448a35bfc1d [ diff ] 
actor: Move implicit origin grant to ProcessNextFromDispatcher

ExecutionEngine implicitly grants the target origin to specific tools
(Navigate) in ExecutionEngine::Act. However, this doesn't get applied to
a Navigate if it's enqueued as a followup from another tool. This
complicates composing Navigate into other tools (such as OpenUrl).

Move this to happen as part of the newly added dispatcher loop on each
new ToolRequest. This obviates the need for composite tools like OpenUrl
to explicitly override AssociatedOriginGrant (which would be impossible
if a tool wanted to issue multiple navigations).

This is ok to do since tools such as this are still an explicit request
from the client model to navigate to the specified origin.

[DevTools] Fix DevTools.BlockedByPolicy.Reason for view-source blocks

DevToolsPolicyDialog::Show() recomputed the block reason from the
WebContents it was given instead of getting it from the check that
blocked. That only works when the check was made on that same tab.

ViewSourceNavigationThrottle blocks on the URL being viewed and then
shows the dialog on the tab doing the view-source load. For Ctrl+U,
"View page source" and "View frame source" that is a new tab that has
not committed anything; for a typed view-source: URL it is a tab whose
committed URL is another page. Recomputing the reason there finds
nothing restricted and falls back to DeveloperToolsAvailability, so
unless that policy was Disallowed these blocks were recorded as "Not
blocked (unexpected)". The blocks themselves were correct; only the
recorded reason was wrong.

Show() now takes the reason from its caller:
- ViewSourceNavigationThrottle passes the reason computed for the URL
  being viewed.
- DevToolsWindow::ToggleDevToolsWindow() passes the reason it already
  checked.
- The view-source and Apple Events JavaScript browser actions and the
  context menu keep blocking on AllowDevToolsFor() and pass
  DevToolsWindow::GetBlockReasonFor() for the same profile and tab, so
  the recorded reason matches the decision.

Regression tests cover the cases that used to record "Not blocked
(unexpected)": a typed view-source: URL on the blocklist, "View frame
source" from the context menu on a blocklisted frame, and view-source of
a policy-installed extension page. Further tests cover
DeveloperToolsAvailability set to Disallowed, an allowed view-source
load, and the reason recorded by the View Source command and the context
menu.

The histogram summary notes the change so that data from before and
after it can be told apart.

Bug: b:538157655
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*DevToolsPolicyDialog*
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*ViewSourceNavigationThrottle*
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*DevToolsPolicyContextMenu*
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=PolicyTest.ViewSourceDisabledBy*

b3b40f59b0c2de3b38253144c8abcde417f04fd3 [ diff ] 
[iOS][Enterprise] Authenticate trailing-dot FQDN URLs for PvD proxies

On iOS, WebKit's native proxy configuration (`nw_proxy_config_t`)
normalizes trailing FQDN dots when evaluating `match_domains`, routing
requests such as `https://example.com.` through the Secure Gateway proxy
configured for `example.com` or `*.example.com`. However,
`NSURLResponse.URL` preserves the trailing dot on the 407 challenge URL,
causing `net::ProxyHostMatchingRules` literal hostname matching in
`enterprise_net::FindMatchingProxyEndpoint()` to miss the route and fall
back to the system proxy sign-in prompt.

Strip a single trailing FQDN dot from non-IP destination hostnames on
iOS when matching PvD proxy endpoints so proxy auth classification
agrees with WebKit's routing (routed and authenticated). Non-iOS
platforms remain unchanged (neither routed nor authenticated); the
cross-platform discrepancy is tracked in crbug.com/590523095.

Include Windows App-Bound Encryption state in DTC signals

During the Device Trust handshake, Context-Aware Access and Verified
Access API v2 callers cannot currently tell whether Chrome on Windows is
protecting browser data with App-Bound Encryption, or why protection
failed to start (such as policy disablement, roaming profiles, an
unsupported install path, or an elevation service error).

Add a Windows signal helper (`app_bound_encryption_state_win.cc`) that
checks whether the async encryptor is actively using the "v20" provider
and falls back to `os_crypt::GetAppBoundEncryptionSupportLevel` only
when "v20" is inactive. Collect this state in `ProfileSignalsCollector`
for `Trigger::kBrowserNavigation` and expose `"appBoundEncryptionState"`
in `BrowserSignalsDecorator` behind the default-disabled
`DeviceTrustAppBoundEncryptionStateSignal` flag.

Bug: 441040062, 581025839
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=AppBoundEncryptionStateWinTest.*
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=ProfileSignalsCollector*
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=*BrowserSignalsDecoratorTest.*
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=DeviceTrustAppBoundBrowserTest.*

[TTC] Enforce PageContextEligibility during actionable page refreshes

In actionable page content mode, speech-turn refreshes call
optimization_guide::GetAIPageContent directly rather than going through
PageContentExtractionService. Previously, OnActionablePageContentFetched
forwarded the extracted content without checking server-upload
eligibility.

Await optimization_guide::PageContextEligibility::GetAsync in
OnActionablePageContentFetched and check
optimization_guide::IsPageContextEligible before emitting refreshed
actionable content, matching the PCES extraction path. Ineligible pages
emit kSafetyPolicy instead of sharing page content.

Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*ServerUploadIneligiblePages*

Skip Dawn Vulkan adapter enumeration on Mesa < 22.0

Re-enabling WebGPU metrics on Linux caused SIGABRTs in Dawn's
LoadInstanceProcs on Mesa < 22.0 (20.3.x/21.x across Intel, AMD, and
Nouveau; none on >= 22.0). Gate Vulkan probing behind a driver bug
workaround, matching the Mesa >= 22.0 floor from crrev.com/c/7486659.

Reland "Add circle buttons to switch prompt endpoints on the selection overlay"

This is a reland of commit 894f61fc4c5c97666a2e701ef80bc7e552243f57

Changes since original CL:

- Reposition the visible floating prompt immediately when
  setPromptEndpoints updates the endpoint list instead of waiting for
  the ResizeObserver to fire after rendering switcher buttons into a
  stale max-width.
- Defer floating prompt repositioning from the ResizeObserver callback
  to requestAnimationFrame whenever hiding the container or changing
  max-width, preventing "ResizeObserver loop completed with undelivered
  notifications." errors when resizing or hiding the observed container.
- Wait for the prompt to settle after setPromptEndpoints in
  PromptBoxExtraRowWidthTriggersRightAlignmentFallback so the
  repositioning is exercised deterministically, and add a unit test
  covering deferred hiding from ResizeObserver.

Original change's description:
> Add circle buttons to switch prompt endpoints on the selection overlay
>
> Instead of cycling prompt endpoints by clicking the endpoint icon inside
> the searchbox pill, provide dedicated circle buttons adjacent to the
> pill for inactive endpoints.
>
> - Make the internal endpoint icon non-interactive while retaining the
>   current active icon image.
> - Render circle switcher buttons for inactive prompt endpoints next to
>   the searchbox pill, supporting arbitrary numbers of endpoints in
>   circular order.
> - Animate endpoint transitions using a slide-in expansion from the
>   selected circle button and a collapsing ghost of the outgoing pill,
>   respecting reduced-motion preferences.
> - Keep keyboard focus inside the prompt input when clicking switcher
>   buttons and preserve input value.
> - Account for switcher button widths in floating prompt positioning and
>   sizing.
> - Add interactive UI and WebUI unit tests covering the new endpoint
>   switcher buttons and animations.
>

tree
398606ebf358c8e14dda0432cb9f7e80eba70cf5
parent
65f95e08dc86dad934f69e17151f2ed3d7991ae3 [ diff ] 
File API: don't revoke file read permission in FileSystemManagerImpl

This code path is used for webkitRequestFileSystem() (legacy sandboxed
file system API), files shared via drag and drop as FileSystemEntry, and
some CrOS extensions APIs. Other paths, like that used for <input
type=file>, never revoke the read access for the render process, so make
FSMI match that behavior.

This was the last/only non-test client of CPSP's revocation API, so
that's also removed.

tree
64829b763be21f3e12c0ab17e90fc8ed03d19f34
parent
1d7caaec57a9c5a68bcfebfd0610ff5d2804ddde [ diff ] 
[TrustedVault] Gate desktop passkeys client on shared storage flag

StandaloneTrustedVaultBackend only supports SecurityDomainId::kPasskeys
when kTrustedVaultSharedRecoveryFactorsAndEncryption is enabled,
matching webauthn::IsLocalGpmPasskeysEnabled(). Previously,
TrustedVaultServiceFactory instantiated the desktop kPasskeys client
whenever device::kWebAuthnLocalGpmPasskeys was enabled, even when
kTrustedVaultSharedRecoveryFactorsAndEncryption was disabled.

Gate desktop passkeys_client creation in TrustedVaultServiceFactory on
both device::kWebAuthnLocalGpmPasskeys and
trusted_vault::kTrustedVaultSharedRecoveryFactorsAndEncryption, and
update the TrustedVaultService comment.

[TrustedVault] Scope ClearLocalDataForAccount to security domain

TrustedVaultService instantiates one TrustedVaultClient per
SecurityDomainId, and IOSTrustedVaultClient already scopes
ClearLocalDataForAccount() to its own security domain. By contrast,
StandaloneTrustedVaultClient::ClearLocalDataForAccount() forwarded only
the account and wiped the entire user entry in LocalDomainsStorage,
deleting vault keys across all security domains along with GPM PIN
metadata and shared physical device private keys.

Pass security_domain_ from StandaloneTrustedVaultClient through
StandaloneTrustedVaultFrontend to StandaloneTrustedVaultBackend, and
replace StandaloneTrustedVaultStorage::ClearDataForUser() with
ClearDomainDataForUser():

- Erase vault keys and recovery factor registrations only for the
  requested security domain, preserving other security domains, GPM PIN
  metadata, shared physical device private keys, and deferred
  non-primary deletion state (skipping disk writes on no-ops in
  LocalDomainsStorage).
- Re-trigger local recovery factor registration only for the cleared
  security domain when clearing the primary account.

Bug: 542895033, 587291121

## wnwen (human)

longer exists. Its remaining CheckOutput call also passed

flags such as _JAVA_OPTIONS if ever invoked.

Remove the unused script, its .pydeps file, and the corresponding entry
from PRESUBMIT.py.

[ios] Add knowledge base rules: documentation-derived (2/2)

This CL is part of a chain that lands the iOS code review knowledge base
under //ios/agents/knowledge_base: Markdown rules that an automated
reviewer evaluates against the added lines of a CL. The philosophy is to
review them, but to err on the side of landing them as reasonably fast
as possible so we can get early feedback on the code review agent's
quality and iterate on individual rules as we go.

The knowledge base is import-only: this chain does not opt //ios into
automated review. A directory that is already opted in (agentic_review
in its DIR_METADATA) gets the rules by adding mixins:
"//ios/agents/knowledge_base/COMMON_METADATA" next to that block.

Rules derived directly from //ios/best_practices.md and
//ios/architecture/ (second half).

Rules in this CL:

  • ios/coordinators-must-be-browser-scoped-not-tab-scoped (chrome,
  should_fix, 0 supporting CLs)
  • ios/earl-grey-tests-must-subclass-chrome-test-case (chrome,
  should_fix, 0 supporting CLs)
  • ios/header-declaration-order-properties-methods-inits (general,
  should_fix, 9 supporting CLs)
  • ios/local-command-protocols-stay-in-feature-ui (chrome, should_fix,
  1 supporting CLs)
  • ios/mediator-class-name-suffix-reserved (chrome, should_fix, 0
  supporting CLs)
  • ios/mediator-deterministic-cleanup-in-disconnect (chrome,
  should_fix, 14 supporting CLs)
  • ios/mediator-observes-web-state-list-for-active-tab (chrome,
  should_fix, 2 supporting CLs)
  • ios/mediator-pushes-state-to-consumer-no-polling (chrome,
  should_fix, 7 supporting CLs)
  • ios/no-auto-type-deduction-for-basic-objective-c-alloc-init
  (general, should_fix, 0 supporting CLs)
  • ios/no-named-cpp-namespaces-in-ios-chrome (chrome, should_fix, 5
  supporting CLs)
  • ios/observe-model-via-scoped-observation-bridges (general,
  should_fix, 4 supporting CLs)
  • ios/prefer-base-time-over-ns-date (general, should_fix, 0 supporting
  CLs)
  • ios/prefer-enum-class-over-ns-enum (general, should_fix, 8
  supporting CLs)
  • ios/prefer-pref-service-over-ns-user-defaults (chrome, should_fix, 1
  supporting CLs)

  supporting CLs)

Linux: Limit MD caption button hit area to the hover circle

On Linux opaque frames (Qt UI, and GTK without native nav buttons),
each caption button covered the whole tab strip row, while its hover
highlight is a 28 dip circle. Clicks in the band below the close
button's circle, just above the toolbar and next to the app menu
button, therefore closed the window.

Size kMdButton caption buttons to their hover circle, so the frame
above and below it stays draggable caption area. Maximized windows
keep extending the buttons to the screen top, and the outermost
button to the screen edge, per Fitts' law; an empty top border keeps
the icon and circle where they are. The tab strip exclusion still
reserves the whole row, so layout and painting are unchanged.

FrameCaptionButton now centers its click ripple and focus ring on its
contents bounds, like the icon and hover circle, so they stay aligned
with the top inset.

Fixed: 40912847

parent
25724302c23d2d40ccd1fb9a967cbc3f5c14579d [ diff ] 
[Linux] QT: Fall back to xcb/offscreen if the wayland plugin is missing

On Wayland, QtUi passes "-platform wayland" to QApplication. The QT
wayland platform plugin is a separate package on Debian and Ubuntu
(qtwayland5 / qt6-wayland), and when it is missing QT calls qFatal(),
aborting the browser as soon as the "Use QT" theme is selected.

Pass "wayland;xcb;offscreen" instead. QT tries each platform in order,
so XWayland is used when available and offscreen otherwise.

Fixed: 583700261

Linux: Show extensions in described file chooser filter names

The GTK and portal file choosers show only a filter's name, so a filter
described as e.g. "Custom Files" gave no hint of which files it
matches. Append the filter's patterns to the description, as the
Windows file dialog does, e.g. "Custom Files (*.text, *.txt)".

[TTC][HOTL][8/N] Add speech answers to HotlValidator

Human-on-the-Loop (HOTL) confirmations also accept spoken "Yes" and "No"
answers through local speech recognition.

- Add an optional RecognizerOptions parameter to HotlValidator's
  constructor holding a Conversation, a HotlSpeechRecognizer, and a
  recognition locale matching the UI strings.
- Route Conversation microphone audio to the recognizer while the
  confirmation is pending, and clear the audio sink when the
  confirmation resolves or destroys the validator.
- Encapsulate localized YES and NO string loading inside
  MatchHotlTranscript. Keep listening on ambiguous transcripts so filler
  words do not abort the prompt. Note for reviewer sign-off: ambient
  speech containing the yes keyword can match within the timeout window.
- Fall back to buttons and the timeout when speech recognition reports
  an error.
- Log TtcHotlSpeechStarted, TtcHotlTranscriptMatched (outcome only,
  never raw text), and TtcHotlSpeechUnavailable in the session journal.

Bug: 560229926, 582755042, 585561006
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter="HotlValidator*:HotlTranscriptMatcherTest.*"

44238f9e48dc33fbf1e3c20832503db09c8dd0cf [ diff ] 
[Sync] Add CrossDeviceTabsService API scaffolding

This CL introduces the public CrossDeviceTabsService interface, data
types, and stub CrossDeviceTabsServiceImpl scaffolding under
components/sync_sessions/ without behavior yet (stub methods call
NOTIMPLEMENTED()).

Centralizing cross-device tab logic behind a KeyedService gives desktop
UIs (Side Panel, device visibility settings, ChroMenu, and macOS tab
menu) a single narrow interface and subscription point. Otherwise, every
cross-device tab UI would need to query OpenTabsUIDelegate directly,
duplicate policy, sign-in, and promo eligibility logic (including
AccountPreviewDataService and visibility prefs), and manage observer and
lifetime wiring across upstream sources (SessionSyncService,
DeviceInfoTracker, SyncService, IdentityManager, visibility prefs, and
policy/sign-in prefs).

fd1f4962ce3302e7593b6e45e62a48477bb77f81
parent
93d3e4c1376c166e72001c3f8c93a16e03eddbb3 [ diff ] 
[DefaultBrowser] Auto-close loss notification on default recovery

Previously, `DefaultBrowserNotificationObserver` overwrote its last
known `IS_DEFAULT` state on `UNKNOWN_DEFAULT` results (causing a
subsequent `NOT_DEFAULT` check to be missed) and left displayed loss
notifications open after Chrome became the default browser again.

Ignore `UNKNOWN_DEFAULT` updates in `DefaultBrowserNotificationObserver`
and automatically close a still-displayed notification when Chrome
returns to `IS_DEFAULT`. When closing the notification on default
recovery, record both
`DefaultBrowserInteractionType::kClosedOnDefaultRestored` (so each
setter's `Interaction` histogram continues to sum to its `Shown` count)
and
`DefaultBrowser.ChangeDetectedNotification.TimeUntilDefaultWithoutClick`
(measuring how long the notification remained displayed before recovery
without a click).

[ash] app_access: take the notifier's user off the active session

UserManager::GetActiveUser() is deprecated in favour of
SessionManager::GetActiveSession(), so the two places that wanted the
active user read it there.

GetActiveUserAccountId() existed to be overridden by the test, which
had its own idea of who was active. The fixture names one user now, so
the seam is gone and the read is inlined at both call sites.

Sort ProductionSupportedFlagList alphabetically on feature name

This CL adds a keep sorted block which selects the feature name and
enforces alphabetical ordering on either the quoted string name or the
feature literal (ignoring the class). So AwFeatures.MY_FEATURE would be
sorted only on "MY_FEATURE" and not "AwFeatures". This matches the
current behaviour of aw_feature_map.cc.

bdeab2e22d3ee13efa9423d4734563de5b43e31a
parent
e61fd0c030615c6d3342b04cf647694e83eff00b [ diff ] 
[TrustedVault] Add EnrollInTrustedVault to SyncUserSettings

Expose EnrollInTrustedVault() on SyncUserSettings so UI surfaces such as
the Password Manager promo can initiate non-interactive Trusted Vault
(on-device encryption) enrollment.

SyncUserSettingsImpl forwards EnrollInTrustedVault() via
SyncUserSettingsImpl::Delegate to SyncServiceImpl, which verifies that
local Sync is disabled, Sync transport is active, the passphrase type is
KEYSTORE_PASSPHRASE, and the primary account has the
is_trusted_vault_passphrase_eligible capability. SyncServiceCrypto then
verifies that no encryption user action is required, invokes
TrustedVaultClient::Enroll(), calls
SyncEngine::SetTrustedVaultEncryptionPassphrase() with the newly
generated key and version, refreshes degraded recoverability state, and
invokes the caller's callback asynchronously.

SyncServiceCrypto also coalesces concurrent
IsTrustedVaultEnrollmentPossible() and EnrollInTrustedVault() callers
and resolves pending callbacks asynchronously with false on Reset(), and
FakeTrustedVaultClient queues CanEnroll() and Enroll() responses
asynchronously.

[Settings] Stop disabling SettingsInTab in Tips promo tests

SettingsInTab and SettingsInTabDesktop will be removed, so tests can no
longer turn them off. Four TipsNotificationsFeaturePromoTest tests do
so to work around an open bug with settings in a tab. They now call
SettingsInTab.setShouldOpenSettingsInTabForTesting(false), which keeps
them on SettingsActivity on every form factor, as before. The TODO
stays.

tree
0831989b6c15a5f6e6f7fea0198c95a0a29727a9
parent
00348bdce3977f25ffdebef14c73a6a7c5b45548 [ diff ] 
[ash] app_access: give the test one idea of who is active

AppAccessNotifierTest brought up a FakeChromeUserManager before
AshTestHelper::SetUp(), so AshTestHelper took its fallback branch and
built a SessionManager with no UserManager bound. The fixture then had
two unrelated notions of the active user: the accounts it switched
between in the FakeChromeUserManager, and whichever user AshTestHelper
had signed in to ash's SessionController. TestAppAccessNotifier existed
to paper over the gap, overriding GetActiveUserAccountId() to return the
fixture's own answer.

It uses UserSessionTestEnvironment with the //chrome delegate now, so
the UserManager, the SessionManager and the SessionController all name
the same two users, and the override is gone. The accounts are
Gaia-typed, which registering a user requires.

The delegate gives each user a real profile, so their AppServiceProxy
registers the app service caches itself and the fixture no longer keeps
its own. That is why AppAccessNotifier is built after the first user is
signed in: it looks the active user's caches up as it is constructed.

#ai-assisted

68ff842e753e032d38506d9636167f8913c1cf27
parent
ea0c48f7fdcfd5db504ad36e6ac4a4b984feb9f3 [ diff ] 
Remove CheckLocaleMode and migrate callers

Previously, l10n_util::CheckAndResolveLocale() accepted a
CheckLocaleMode enum so callers that could not perform disk I/O could
pass kUseKnownLocalesList instead of verifying resource packs on disk.

Migrate all kUseKnownLocalesList callers to query
ui_l10n::GetPlatformLanguageMatcher().Match() directly and remove
CheckLocaleMode, leaving CheckAndResolveLocale() as a disk-backed
helper.
